((1. nūṉmarapu))

iv= ōttu eṉ nutaliṟṟu* ō ~eṉiṉ, atu ~um ataṉ peyar uraippa ~ē aṭaṅkum. iv= atikārattāṟ collappaṭum eḻuttu*-ilakkaṇattiṉai ōrāṟṟāl tokuttu uṇarttutaliṉ, nūṉmarapu eṉṉum peyarttu. itaṉuḷ+ kūṟukiṉṟa ilakkaṇam moḻi ~iṭai (niṉṟa) eḻuttiṟku* aṉṟi+ taṉi-niṉṟa eḻuttiṟku* eṉa uṇarka.

((1. eḻuttukkaḷiṉ vakai))

TE1i

{Z 1}

{C TOL}
eḻuttu* eṉappaṭupa
akara mutal ṉakara iṟuvāy
muppaḵtu* eṉpa
cārntu-varal marapiṉ mūṉṟu* alaṅkaṭai ~ē.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} i+ talaiccūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟu* ō ~eṉiṉ eḻuttukkaḷatu peyar -um muṟai -~um tokai -~um uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}eḻuttu eṉappaṭupa{/T}
eḻuttu* eṉṟu ciṟappittu+ collappaṭuvaṉa
{T}akaram mutal ṉakara iṟuvāy muppaḵtu eṉpa{/T}
akaram ākiya mutalai ~uṭaiyaṉa -~um ṉakaram ākiya iṟuvāyiṉai ~uṭaiyaṉa -~um ākiya muppatu* eṉṟu collupa (āciriyar)
{T}cārntu -varal marapiṉ mūṉṟu alaṅkaṭai{/T}
cārntu varutal ākiya ilakkaṇattiṉai ~uṭaiya mūṉṟu* um allāviṭattu.

mūṉṟu* -um āṉa -~iṭattu muppattu-mūṉṟu eṉṟu collupa eṉṟavāṟu.

e-ṭu: a-ā-i-ī-u-ū-e-ē-ai-o-ō-au; k-ṅ-c-ñ-ṭ-ṇ-t-n-p-m-y-r-l-v-ḻ-ḷ-ṟ-ṉ eṉa varum.

{T}eṉappaṭupa{/T} eṉṟa ciṟappāṉ, aḷapeṭai -~um uyirmey= -um varivaṭivu* -um ciṟappu* illā eḻuttu* āka+ koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. a ā eṉpaṉa peyar: muṟai, a+ muṟai; tokai muppatu; avaṟṟuḷ, akaram tāṉ -um iyaṅki+ taṉimeykaḷai iyakkutal+ ciṟappāṉ, muṉ vaikkappaṭṭatu. ṉakāram vīṭu-pēṟṟiṟku* uriya āṇ-pālai uṇarttutal+ ciṟappāṉ piṉ vaikka+-paṭṭatu.

tokai (~eṉpatu) tokaiyuḷtokai -~um, tokaiyuḷvakai -~um, tokaiyuḷviri -~um, vakaiyuḷtokai -~um, vakaiyuḷvakai -~um, vakaiyuḷviri -~um, viriyuḷtokai -~um, viriyuḷvakai -~um, viriyuḷviri -~um eṉa oṉpatu vakaippaṭum.

eḻuttu* eṉpatu tokaiyuḷtokai, muppatu* eṉpatu ataṉ vakai. muppattumūṉṟu* eṉpatu ataṉ viri. muppatu* eṉpatu vakaiyuḷtokai. muppattumūṉṟu* eṉpatu ataṉ vakai. aḷapeṭai talaippeytu nāṟpatu* eṉpatu ataṉ viri. muppattumūṉṟu* eṉpatu viriyuḷtokai. nāṟpatu* eṉpatu ataṉ vakai. uyirmey talaippeytu irunūṟṟaimpattāṟu* eṉpatu ataṉ viri. ceyyuḷiṉpam nōkki vakaram nīkki+ pakaram iṭappaṭṭatu. akara mutal ṉakara ~iṟuvāy eṉṉa, irupeyaroṭṭu* ākupeyarāṉ muppataṉ mēl niṉṟaṉa.

e-ṭu: {{kākkai, eṅṅaṉam, paccai, maññai, paṭṭai, maṇṇai, tattai, vennōy, appai, ammi, veyyar, elli, evvi, koḷḷi, koṟṟi, kaṉṉi}} eṉa varum. {T}meynnilai cuṭṭiṉ{/T} eṉṟataṉāl, {T}tam muṉ tām varum{/T} eṉṟatu, mey+ muṉṉar mey= eṉṉum māttirai ~aṉṟi, uṭaṉilai mey mēlatu* ām eṉpatu koḷka. {T}ellām{/T} eṉṟatu, mēl ya-ra-ḻa eṉṟa atikāram māṟṟi vantu -niṉṟatu.

ōreḻuttu* āy niṉṟu oru moḻi ~āy niṟaital ila.

avaṟṟuḷ+ cila niṟaikkum.

okaram oḻinta nāṉku*um cuṭṭu* āy um viṉā ~āy um moḻi niṟaikkum aṉṟu* ō ~eṉiṉ, avai iṭaiccol= ātaliṉ, avaṟṟiṟku+ karuvi ceyyār eṉka. itu ~um uyirkku* um uyirmeykku* um potu. aintu* um eṉpataṉ ummai īṇṭu eccappaṭa niṉṟatu.

e-ṭu. {{tu, no}} eṉa varum.

((cakara-mey oṉpatu* uyiruṭaṉ moḻi-mutal ātal))

a ai au eṉṉum mūṉṟu* -um allā -~iṭattu.

e-ṭu: {{cālai cilai cīṟuka curumpu cūḻka ceykai cēvaṭi coṟika cōṟu}} eṉa varum.

{U 3} {Z 0}

((3. piṟappiyal))

((eḻuttukkaḷiṉ piṟappilakkaṇam uṇarttuvatu))

iv= ōttu eṉṉa peyarttu* ō ~eṉiṉ, eḻuttukkaḷatu piṟappu uṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ piṟappiyal eṉṉum peyarttu. itaṉai nūṉmarapiṉ piṉ= ē vaikka ~eṉiṉ, cārpiṟṟōṟṟatteḻuttu* um taṉimey= um moḻimarapiṭai uṇartti+ [piṟappu] uṇartta vēṇṭutaliṉ, moḻimarapiṉ piṉṉatu* āyiṟṟu.

{^L}

{T}nuvaṉṟiciṉ{/T}

maṟṟu, iḵtu {T}aḷapiṟ kōṭal antaṇar maṟaittu{/T} (([E102i])) eṉa ~ē peṟappaṭṭatu* aṉṟu* ō ~eṉiṉ, {T}antaṇar maṟaittu{/T} eṉpatu piṟaṉkōḷ+ kūṟutaṟku* um, piṟaṉkōḷ+ kūṟṟu nērntu uṭampaṭutaṟku* um oppa+ kiṭantamaiyiṉ av= aiyam tīrtaṟku+ kūṟiṉār eṉpatu. puṟattu icaippatal% muṉṉar, akattu icaikkum vaḷi ~icaiyai a+ maṟaikkaṇ ōr eḻuttiṟku mūṉṟu nilai uḷatu* āka+ kūṟum (ataṉ āciriyaṉ) aḵtu āmāṟu aṟintukoḷka. {T}mey teri{/T} eṉṟataṉāṉ, muṟku mutaliyaṉa muyaṟciyāṟ piṟakkum eṉiṉum, poruḷ teriyā nilaimaiya ~ākaliṉ avaṟṟiṟku aḷapu kūṟār āyiṉār eṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu. {T}nuvaṉṟiciṉ{/T} eṉpatu īṇṭu iṟantakāla+ taṉmaiviṉai.

((mūṉṟāvatu piṟappiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.))

((4. puṇariyal))

{INTRO} i+ talai+ cūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟu* ō ~eṉiṉ, moḻimarapiṉuḷ mutal um īṟu* um kūṟiyavaḻi uyir um mey= um uyirmey= um eṉa mūṉṟu* āy virintuniṉṟataṉai uyir um mey= um eṉa iraṇṭu* āka+ tokuttalāṉ um, avvaḻi irupattiraṇṭu eḻuttu moḻikku mutal, irupattunāṉku eḻuttu moḻikku īṟu eṉa+ kūṟaliṉ, muppattumūṉṟu eṉappaṭṭa eḻuttu nāṟpattāṟu āvaṉa pōla virintataṉai avai ~ellām muppattumūṉṟiṉ uḷḷaṉa ~ē eṉa+ tokuttalāṉ um virintatu tokuttal nutaliṟṟu.

{T}oḻukkal valiya{/T} eṉṟataṉāṉ, eḻuttu* um cāriyai ~um uṭaṉ peṟutal koḷka.

{{avaṟṟukkōṭu, kalattukkuṟai}} eṉa varum.

alvaḻi muṟ kūṟātatu, vēṟṟumai ~allātatu alvaḻi ~eṉa vēṇṭaliṉ eṉpatu, muṉ= ē `puṇarmoḻi' eṉṟu vaittu, `puṇaruṅkālai' eṉṟataṉāṟ puṇarccikkaṇ= ē vēṟṟumai alvaḻi eṉa iraṇṭāvatu, allākkāl, vēṟṟumai ~eṉa ~ē paṭum eṉpatu koḷka. īṇṭu alvaḻi ~eṉṟatu perumpāṉmai ~um eḻuvāyiṉai.

((3. urupu puṇarcci))

((vēṟṟumai ~urupukaḷ))

TE114i

{Z 11}

{C TOL}
ai oṭu ku iṉ atu kaṇ= eṉṉum
av= āṟu* eṉpa vēṟṟumai ~urupu* ē.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, mēl vēṟṟumai ~eṉṉappaṭṭa avaṟṟatu peyar um, muṟai ~um, tokai ~um uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}ai oṭu ku iṉ atu kaṇ eṉṉum{/T}
ai oṭu ku iṉ atu kaṇ eṉṟu collappaṭum
{T}a āṟu eṉpa vēṟṟumai urupu{/T}
av= āṟu* um eṉṟu colluvar vēṟṟumai ~urupukaḷai.

iv= āṟu* um allaṉa ~ellām alvaḻi eṉappaṭum. avai eḻuvāy um, viḷi ~um, uvamattokai ~um, ummaittokai ~um, paṇputtokai ~um, irupeyaroṭṭuppaṇputtokai ~um, muṟṟu* um, iru vakai eccam um, iṭai ~um, uri ~um eṉa ivai.

((valimutal urupu puṇarunilai))

TE115i

{C TOL}

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, nāṉkāvataṟku* um ēḻāvataṟku* um urupiyalai nōkkiṟṟu* ōr karuvi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}valleḻuttu mutaliya vēṟṟumai urupiṟku{/T}

{T}olvaḻi oṟṟu iṭai mikutal vēṇṭum{/T}
poruntiṉa iṭattu valloṟṟu* āyiṉum melloṟṟu* āyiṉum iṭaikkaṇ mikutal vēṇṭum.

e-ṭu: {{ūrkku, nīrkku, ūrkkaṇ nīrkkaṇ}} eṉa valleḻuttu mikkaṉa. {{taṅkaṇ, eṅkaṇ}} eṉa melleḻuttu mikkaṉa.

`olvaḻi' eṉṟataṉāl, {{aracarkaṇ, pārppārkaṇ}} eṉa oṟṟu mikātaṉa ~um koḷka. "meypiṟitātal" (([E110i])) mutalāya nāṉku puṇarcci ~um urupu puṇarccikkaṇ= um eytaliṉ, meypiṟitu* ātalai eṭuttōtātu mikkataṉai eṭuttōtiyavataṉāṉ, mikka puṇarcci ~allaṉa ~um īṇṭu* ē koḷka. {{poṟku, poṟkaṇ, āṅkaṇ, īṅkaṇ, ūṅkaṇ, avaṟku, ivaṟku, nuṅkaṇ, koṟṟaṟku, cāttaṟku}} eṉa varum.

{{avaṉ kaṇ, cāttaṉkaṇ}} eṉpuḻi iyalpu* um itaṉāṉ ē koḷka.

((atu urupiṉ akaram keṭum))

«eṭutta naṟaviṉ kulaiyalam^ kāntaḷ»
(([ANONYME]))

eṉpuḻi `alam' eṉpatu* ōr cāriyai ~um uṇṭu* āl eṉiṉ, ataṉai «alaṅku kāntaḷ» eṉpataṉ vikāram eṉpa. iṉ cāriyai vaḻakku+ payiṟciyāṉ um nūluḷ+ palakālum eṭuttōtappaṭalāṉ um, vāḷā ōtiyavaḻi+ tāṉ ē cēṟalāṉ um muṉ vaikkappaṭṭatu. aṉ cāriyai ~um atu pōla+ ciṟappu* uṭaimaiyiṉ piṉ vaikkappaṭṭatu. iṭai niṉṟavaṟṟu* iyalpu* um aṟintukoḷka.

e-ṭu: {{puḷiyaṅkōṭu, puḷiyañcetiḷ, puḷiyantōl}} eṉa varum.

{T}meymmai ~ākalum uṟaḻa+ tōṉṟal um a muṟai iraṇṭu* um uriyavai uḷa{/T}
iyalpu* ākal um uṟaḻa+ tōṉṟutal um ākiya a+ muṟaimaiyiṉai ~uṭaiya iraṇṭu ceykai ~um uriyaṉa uḷa
{T}vēṟṟumai maruṅkiṉ pōṟṟal vēṇṭum{/T}
avaṟṟai mēl ē vēṟṟumai+ poruṭpuṇarcci muṭipu collumiṭattu+ pōṟṟiyaṟital vēṇṭum.

e-ṭu: {{nāykōṭpaṭṭāṉ, pulikōṭpaṭṭāṉ, cārappaṭṭāṉ, tīṇṭappaṭṭāṉ, pāyappaṭṭāṉ}} eṉa ivai iyalpu. {{cūrkōṭpaṭṭāṉ, cūrkkōṭpaṭṭāṉ, vaḷikōṭpaṭṭāṉ, vaḷikkōṭpaṭṭāṉ}} eṉa ivai uṟaḻcci.

puḷḷiyīṟu uyirīṟu eṉṟataṉāl, {{pēeykōṭpaṭṭāṉ, pēey+ kōṭpaṭṭāṉ}} eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyuḷ ēkara+ pēṟu koḷka. {T}uriyavaiyuḷa{/T} eṉṟataṉāṉ, {{pāmpukōṭpaṭṭāṉ, pāppukkōṭpāṭṭāṉ}} eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyuḷ nilaimoḻi ~oṟṟu+ tiriyāmai ~um tirital um koḷka.

uḷa ~eṉappaṭṭa ellā+ col= um
tattam^ kiḷavi tam= akappaṭṭa
muttai varūum^ kālam^ tōṉṟiṉ
ottatu* eṉpa ē ~eṉ cāriyai.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, aḷavuppeyar um niṟaippeyar um eṇṇuppeyar um tammiṟ puṇarumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}uyir um puḷḷi ~um iṟuti ~āki aḷavu* um niṟai ~um eṇṇum cuṭṭi uḷa eṉappaṭṭa ellā collum{/T}

{T}tam tam kiḷavi tam akappaṭṭa muttai varum kālam tōṉṟiṉ ē eṉ cāriyai ottatu eṉpa{/T}
tamakku iṉam ākiya coṟkaḷ āy+ tamakku akappaṭum moḻi ~āy uḷḷaṉa tam muṉ= ē varum kālam tōṉṟum āyiṉ ē eṉṉum^ cāriyai peṟṟu muṭitalai+ poruntiṟṟu eṉpa.

e-ṭu: {{uḻakkēyāḻākku, kalaṉēpatakku}} eṉa ivai aḷavu+ peyar. {{toṭiyēkaḵcu, koḷḷē aiyavi}} eṉa ivai niṟaippeyar. {{kāṇiyēmuntirikai, kālēkāṇi}} eṉa ivai eṇṇuppeyar.

uyirīṟu puḷḷiyīṟu eṉṟataṉāṉ ē, ē eṉ cāriyai peṟātaṉa ~um uḷa ~eṉṟu koḷka. {{kuṟuṇi nāṉāḻi}} eṉa varum.

`tōṉṟal' eṉṟataṉāṉ, {{evaṉ}} eṉṟu* um, {{eṉ}} eṉṟu* um niṟutti, vaṟṟu+ koṭuttu vēṇṭum ceykai ceytu {{evaṟṟai, evaṟṟoṭu}} eṉa ~um, {{eṟṟai, eṟṟoṭu}} eṉa ~um muṭikka.

`ottatu' eṉṟataṉāl, {{ekiṉ}} eṉa niṟutti, attu* um iṉ= um koṭuttu+ ceykai ceytu {{ekiṉattai, ekiṉattoṭu, ekiṉiṉai, ekiṉiṉoṭu}} eṉa ~um muṭikka.

attu muṉ+ kūṟiyavataṉāṉ, attu+ peṟṟavaḻi iṉitu icaikkum eṉa+ koḷka.

{{aḻaṉ}}

piṇam.

((1. akara īṟu))

{C URAI}

{INTRO} i+ talaiccūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟu* ō ~eṉiṉ, akaravīṟṟu+ peyarkku vaṉkaṇattōṭu alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}akara iṟuti+ peyarnilai muṉṉar{/T}

{T}vēṟṟumai alvaḻi+ ka-ca-ta-pa+ tōṉṟiṉ{/T}
vēṟṟumai ~allāta alvaḻikkaṇ ka-ca-ta-pa mutal moḻi varumoḻi ~āy+ tōṉṟiṉ
{T}tam tam otta oṟṟu iṭaimikum{/T}

`otta' oṟṟu* eṉṉātu `tattam' eṉṟataṉāṉ, akaravīṟṟu uriccol valleḻuttu mikku* um melleḻuttu mikku* um muṭiyum muṭipu* um, iṭaiccoṟkaḷuḷ eṭuttōtāvaṟṟiṉ muṭivu* um, akaram taṉṉai uṇaraniṉṟavaḻi muṭiyum muṭipu* um koḷka.

{{taṭakkai, tavakkoṇṭāṉ}} eṉa ivai uriccol valleḻuttuppēṟu, {{taṭañcevi, taṭantōḷ}} eṉa ivai melleḻuttu+ pēṟu.
«maṭava maṉṟa taṭavu nilaikkoṉṟai»
(([kuṟun:66_1]))

eṉpatu iṭaiccol muṭipu. {{akkuṟitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu}} eṉpatu taṉṉai uṇara niṉṟavaḻi valleḻuttu mikuti. {{avyātu}} eṉpatu* um iṭaiyeḻuttu mikuti. {{avvaḻakitu}} eṉpataṟku uyirkkaṇattu muṭipu.

e-ṭu: {{avyāḻ, avvaḷai}} eṉa varum.

{C TOL}

{C URAI}

{WFW}

e-ṭu: {{avvāṭai, avvāṇṭai}} eṉa oṭṭuka.

iṭaimikka vakarattiṉai "neṟiyiyal" eṉṟa ilēciṉāṉ iraṭṭutal kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, atu nilaimoḻi+ toḻil eṉpatu ~ē kūṟappaṭṭatu. `tiriyātu' eṉṟataṉāṉ ē, mēṟ cuṭṭu nīṇṭavaḻi yakarattōṭu koḷka.

((ceyyuḷil cuṭṭu nīḷutal))

TE211i

{Z 8}

{C TOL}
aṉṉa eṉṉum uvama+ kiḷavi ~um
aṇmai cuṭṭiya viḷinilai+ kiḷavi ~um
ceymmaṉa eṉṉum^ toḻil iṟu col= um
ēval kaṇṇiya viyaṅkōḷ+ kiḷavi ~um
ceyta eṉṉum peyar eñcu kiḷavi ~um
ceyyiya eṉṉum viṉai ~eñcu kiḷavi ~um
amma eṉṉum urai+ poruḷ+ kiḷavi ~um
palavaṟṟu* iṟuti+ peyar+ koṭai uḷappaṭa
aṉṟi aṉaittum iyalpu* eṉa moḻipa.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, akara ~īṟṟu* uḷ orucār peyarkku* um viṉaikku* um iṭaikku* um muṉ eytiyatu vilakki ~um eytātatu eytuvittu* um muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu. aṉṉa ~eṉpatu* um ceyyiya ~eṉpatu* um {T}palavaṟṟu* iṟuti+ peyar+ koṭai{/T} ~eṉpatu* um eytiyatu vilakkiṉa. maṟṟaiyaṉa eytātatu eytuvittaṉa.

{WFW}
{T}aṉṉa eṉṉum uvamakkiḷavi ~um{/T}
aṉṉa eṉṟu collappaṭum uvama urupu* ākiya iṭaiccol= um
{T}aṇmai cuṭṭiya viḷinilai+ kiḷavi ~um{/T}
aṇiyārai+ karutiya viḷi ~ākiya nilaimaiyai ~uṭaiya uyartiṇai+ peyarccol= um
{T}ceymmaṉa eṉṉum toḻil iṟu col= um{/T}
ceymmaṉa eṉṟu collappaṭum viṉaiccol= ākiya akaravīṟṟu+ col= um
{T}ēval kaṇṇiya viyaṅkōḷ kiḷavi ~um{/T}
ēvalai+ karutiya viyaṅkōḷ ākiya viṉaiccol= um
{T}ceyta eṉṉum peyar-eñcu-kiḷavi ~um{/T}
ceyta eṉṟu collappaṭum peyareccam ākiya viṉaiccol= um
{T}ceyyiya eṉṉum viṉai eñcu kiḷavi ~um{/T}
ceyyiya eṉṟu collappaṭum viṉaiyeccam ākiya viṉaiccol= um
{T}amma eṉṉum uraipporuḷ kiḷavi ~um{/T}
amma eṉṟu collappaṭum uraiyacai+ poruṇmaiyai ~uṭaiya iṭaiccol= um
{T}palavaṟṟu iṟuti+ peyarkkoṭai uḷappaṭa aṉṟi aṉaittum{/T}
paṉmai+ poruḷkaḷiṉ akara ~īṟṟu+ pala ~eṉṉum peyarccol= um ākiya av= aṉaittu+ col= um
{T}iyalpu eṉa moḻipa{/T}
iyalpu* āy muṭiyum eṉṟu colluvar (āciriyar.)

e-ṭu: {{poṉ aṉṉa kutirai, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi}} eṉa ~um; {{ūra koḷ, cel, tā, pō}} eṉa ~um; {{uṇmaṉa kutirai, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi}} eṉa ~um; {{celka kutirai, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi}} eṉa ~um; {{uṇṭa kutirai, cennāy takar, paṉṟi}} eṉa ~um; itaṉ etirmaṟai {{uṇṇāta kutirai}} eṉa ~um; itaṉ kuṟippu {{nalla kutirai, cennāy}} eṉa ~um; {{uṇṇiya koṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ}} eṉa ~um; {{amma koṟṟā, cāttā, tēvā, pūtā}} eṉa ~um; {{pala kutirai, cennāy, takar, paṉṟi}} eṉa ~um varum.

{{ūra koḷ}} eṉpatu uyirīṟu* ākiya uyartiṇaippeyar eṉpataṉ uḷ aṭaṅkātu* ō ~eṉiṉ, {{ūrāṉ}} eṉpatu {{ūra}} eṉa īṟu tirintamaiyiṉ āṇṭu aṭaṅkātu* āyiṟṟu* eṉpatu.

{T}viḷinilaikkiḷavi{/T} ~ākiya peyarai muṉ vaittataṉāl, ceyta ~eṉpataṉ maṟai ~aṉṟi+ ceyyum eṉpataṟku maṟai ~ākiya ceyyāta eṉpatu* um av= iyalpu uṭaittu* eṉa+ koḷka. atu {{vārāta koṟṟaṉ}} eṉa varum.

`uraiyiṟ kōṭal' eṉpataṉāl, viyaṅkōḷ muṉ vaikkal+ pālataṉai muṉ vaiyātu ceymmaṉa eṉpataṉai muṉ vaittataṉāṉ, iv= iyalpu muṭipiṉkaṇ ceymmaṉa eṉpatu ciṟappu* uṭaittu* eṉa+ peṟappaṭṭatu. ataṉāl, `ēvalkaṇṇāta viyaṅkōḷ um' iv= iyalpu-muṭipu uṭaittu* eṉa+ koḷka. atu,
«maṉṉiya peruma nī»
(([puṟam:6_29]))

eṉa varum.

{T}palavaṟṟu* iṟuti+ peyar+ koṭai{/T} eṉpataṉai amma eṉpataṟku muṉ vaiyātataṉāl, pala eṉpatu mēṟkūṟum ceyyuḷ muṭipil tirintu muṭital īṇṭai iyalpiṟ ciṟappiṉṟu* eṉpatūum, akaravīṟṟuḷ muṭipu kūṟāta muṟṟuviṉai ~um viṉaikkuṟippu* um av= iyalpiṉa ~eṉpatūum peṟappaṭum. {{uṇṭaṉa kutirai}} eṉpatu muṟṟuviṉai. {{kariya kutirai}} eṉpatu muṟṟuviṉaikkuṟippu.

`taṉṉiṉa muṭittal' eṉpataṉāl, pala eṉpatiṉ iṉam ākiya cila eṉpataṟku* um av= iyalpu koḷka. {{cila kutirai}} eṉa varum.

((`vāḻiya' eṉṉum col))

{WFW}
{T}palavaṟṟu iṟuti nīṭum moḻi uḷa{/T}

{T}ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻiyāṉa{/T}

`ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya moḻi' eṉṉātu {T}toṭarmoḻi{/T} eṉṟataṉāṉ, {{ippala}} ~eṉpatu nīḷumvaḻi varumoḻi ~āvatu {{cila}} ~eṉpatu* ē eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum. `ceyyuḷāṉa' eṉṉātu {T}ceyyuḷ kaṇṇiya toṭarmoḻiyāṉa{/T} eṉṟataṉāl, {{pala}} eṉṉum moḻi ~īṟu nīṇṭavaḻi nilaimoḻi akarappēṟu* um varumoḻi ñakāra melleḻuttuppēṟu* um, varumoḻi ~īṟu nīṇṭavaḻi akarappēṟu* um makaram ākiya melleḻuttu+ pēṟu* um koḷka. `uṇṭu' eṉṉātu {T}uḷa{/T} eṉṟataṉāl, cila ~eṉṉum varumoḻi ~iṟuti nīṭal um koḷka.

`lakaram ṟakaram ām' eṉṉātu {T}lakaram ṟakara ~oṟṟu* ām{/T} eṉṟa ñāpakattāṉ, akaram keṭum eṉṟāṉ āka+ koḷka. aruttāpatti mukattāl, {T}tam muṉ tām variṉ lakaram ṟakara ~oṟṟu* ām{/T} eṉa ~ē, tam muṉ piṟa vantaviṭattu lakaram ṟakara ~oṟṟu* ākātu akaram keṭum eṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum. {{palpaṭai, palyāṉai, cilpaṭai, cilvēḷvi}} eṉa varum.

{T}toṭar al iṟuti{/T} eṉpatu cuṭṭu* allatu ōreḻuttorumoḻi akaram iṉmaiyiṉ, ōreḻuttorumoḻi mēṟ cellātu* āyiṟṟu. urittu* eṉṟatu, akaravīṟṟu* orumai paṟṟi.

{T}avaṇ{/T} eṉṟataṉāl, {{makappāl yāṭu}} eṉa valleḻuttu+ pēṟu* um, {{makaviṉkai}} eṉa mēl iṉ cāriyai peṟṟavaḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻvu* um, urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ vantavaḻi {{viḷaviṉkōṭu}} eṉa iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻvu* um koḷka. `nilai' eṉṟataṉāl, {{makampālyāṭu}} eṉa melleḻuttu+ pēṟu* um koḷka.

((akara īṟṟu+ paṉmai+ coṟkaḷ))

{WFW}
{T}kuṟiyataṉ iṟuticciṉai keṭa ukaram aṟiya varutal ceyyuḷuḷ urittu{/T}
kuṟiyataṉ iṟutikkaṇ niṉṟa ākārattiṉatu ciṉai ~ākiya akāra māttirai keṭa āṇṭu ukaram aṟiyavarutal ceyyuḷiṭattu urittu.

e-ṭu:
«iṟavu+ puṟattu* aṉṉa piṇar paṭu taṭavu mutal»
(([naṟṟiṇai:19_1]))

eṉa varum.

`aṟiya' eṉṟataṉāṉ, ukaram peṟātu ciṉai keṭutal um koḷka.

{{piṇavunāy muṭukkiya}} eṉṟāṟpōla varum alvaḻi muṭipu {T}kiḷanta ~alla{/T} (([E483i])) eṉṉum puṟaṉaṭaiyatu* eṉa+ koḷka.

((3. ikara īṟu))

TE236i

{Z 33}

{C TOL}
ikara iṟuti+ peyarnilai muṉṉar
vēṟṟumai ~āyiṉ valleḻuttu mikum ē.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, ikaravīṟṟu+ peyarkku alvaḻi muṭipu tokaimarapiṉuṭ kūṟi niṉṟamaiyiṉ ataṉ vēṟṟumai muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}ikara iṟuti+ peyarnilai muṉṉar vēṟṟumai ~āyiṉ valleḻuttu mikum{/T}
ikaravīṟṟu+ peyarccol muṉṉar atikārattāṟ ka-ca-ta-pa mutalmoḻi vantavaḻi vēṟṟumai+ poruṭpuṇarcci ~āyiṉ tamakku+ poruntiṉa valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum.

e-ṭu: {{kiḷikkāl; ciṟaku, talai, puṟam}} eṉa varum.

((ikara īṟṟu viṉaiccol= um iṭaiccol= um))

TE237i

{Z 34}

{C TOL}

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, iv= īṟṟuḷ cila iṭaiccol= um viṉaiccol= um muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.

{T}iṉi aṇi eṉṉum kālai ~um iṭaṉ um viṉaiyeñcu kiḷavi ~um cuṭṭu* um aṉṉa{/T}

e-ṭu: {{iṉikkoṇṭāṉ, aṇikkoṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ}} eṉavum; {{tēṭikkoṇṭāṉ, ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ}} eṉa ~um; {{ikkoṟṟaṉ, cāttaṉ, tēvaṉ, pūtaṉ}} eṉa ~um varum.

((`iṉṟi' eṉṉum col))

TE238i

{Z 35}

{C TOL}
iṉṟi ~eṉṉum viṉaiyeñcu* iṟuti
niṉṟa ikaram ukaram ātal
toṉṟu* iyal maruṅkiṉ ceyyuḷuḷ urittu* ē.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, iv= īṟṟu* uḷ viṉaiyeccakkuṟippiṉ uḷ oṉṟaṟku+ ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}iṉṟi eṉṉum viṉaiyeñcu iṟuti niṉṟa ikaram ukaram ātal{/T}

{T}toṉṟu iyal maruṅkiṉ ceyyuḷuḷ urittu{/T}
paḻaka naṭanta kūṟṟai ~uṭaiya ceyyuṭkaḷiṭattu urittu.

e-ṭu:
«uppiṉṟu puṟkai ~uṇkamā koṟkaiyōṉē»
(([ANONYME]))

eṉa varum.

«nāḷaṉṟu pōki+ puḷḷiṭai taṭpa»
(([puṟam:124_1]))
eṉa varum.

((cuṭṭuppeyar))

{C TOL}

eṉṟatu meṉkaṇam varumvaḻi a+ melleḻuttu mikkum (([E206i])) iṭaikkaṇam varumvaḻi ~um uyirkkaṇam varum vaḻi ~um nilaimoḻi vakaram peṟṟu* um (([E207i, E208i])) ceyyuṭkaṇ vakaram keṭṭu+ cuṭṭu nīṇṭu* um (([E209i])) muṭiyum eṉṟavāṟu.

((`tūṇi' muṉ `patakku'))

TE240i

{C TOL}

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, iv= īṟṟu alvaḻikaḷil aḷavuppeyaruḷ oṉṟaṟku mēl tokai marapiṉuḷ (([E165i])) eytiya ē eṉ cāriyai vilakki vēṟu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.

e-ṭu: {{tūṇippatakku}} eṉa varum.

varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāl, {{irutūṇippatakku}} eṉa aṭaiyaṭuttu vantavaḻi ~um iv viti koḷka. {T}kiḷanteṭutta{/T} eṉṟataṉāl, tūṇi muṉṉar+ piṟa poruṭpeyar vantavaḻi ~um āṇṭu nilaimoḻi ~aṭai ~aṭuttu vantavaḻi ~um taṉ muṉṉar+ tāṉ vantavaḻi ~um i+ muṭipu koḷka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉ ē, taṉ muṉṉar+ tāṉ vantavaḻi ikku+ cāriyai+ pēṟu* um koḷka.

e-ṭu: {{tūṇikkoḷ, tūṇiccāmai}} eṉa ~um; {{iru tūṇi+ koḷ}} eṉa ~um; {{tūṇittūṇi}} eṉa ~um; {{tūṇikkuttūṇi}} eṉa ~um varum.

iṭattiṉai varaintuṇarttum mī eṉṉum col muṉ iyalpu* āy muṭital ē ~aṉṟi valleḻuttu mikku muṭiyum tammil iyaintu niṟṟalai ~uṭaiya moḻikaḷ um uḷa ~eṉṟu colluvar.

e-ṭu: {{mīkkōḷ, mīppal}} eṉa varum.

{T}uṭaṉilai{/T} eṉṟataṉāl, {{mīṅkuḻi, mīntōl}} eṉa melleḻuttu+ peṟṟu muṭivaṉa ~um koḷka.

((vēṟṟumaiyil īkāra īṟṟu+ peyar))

TE253i

{Z 50}

{C TOL}
vēṟṟumaikkaṇ= um ataṉ ōraṟṟu* ē.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, av= īṟṟu+ peyar vēṟṟumaikkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}vēṟṟumaikkaṇ= um ataṉ ōr aṟṟu{/T}
īkāravīṟṟu+ peyar vēṟṟumaipporuḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇ= um ākāravīṟṟu alvaḻiyōṭu orutaṉmaittu* āy valleḻuttu vantavaḻi valleḻuttu+ peṟṟu muṭiyum.

e-ṭu: {{īkkāl; ciṟaku, talai, puṟam}} eṉa varum.

((`nī' eṉṉum peyar))

{T}ā vayiṉ valleḻuttu iyaṟkai ākum{/T}
avvāṟu muṭintaviṭattu iyaipu valleḻuttu mikātu.

((`eru' `ceru' eṉṉum coṟkaḷ))

TE261i

{Z 58}

{C TOL}
eru ~um^ ceru ~um ammoṭu civaṇi+
tiripiṭaṉ uṭaiya teriyum^ kālai

tam= oṟṟu mikūum valleḻuttu* iyaṟkai.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, iv= īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttu vilakki+ cāriyai viti ~um, oṉṟaṟku valleḻuttiṉōṭu cāriyai viti ~um kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}eru ~um ceru ~um ammoṭu civaṇi+ tiripu iṭaṉ uṭaiya teriyum kālai{/T}

{T}ammiṉ makaram ceru vayiṉ keṭum{/T}

{T}valleḻuttu iyaṟkai tam oṟṟu mikūum{/T}

e-ṭu: {{eruvaṅkuḻi; cēṟu, tāṟu, pūḻi}} eṉa ~um; {{ceruvakkaḷam; cēṉai, tāṉai, paṟai}} eṉa ~um varum.

{T}teriyuṅkālai{/T} eṉṟataṉāl, eru eṉpataṟku+ perumpāṉmai melleḻuttu+ pēṟu* um, ciṟupāṉmai valleḻuttu+ pēṟu* um, ceru eṉpataṟku+ ciṟupāṉmai valleḻuttu+ pēṟu* um koḷka. {{eruṅkuḻi, erukkuḻi, cerukkaḷam}} eṉa varum.

{T}valleḻuttiyaṟkai{/T} eṉṟataṉāṉ, urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi av= iru moḻikku* um atikāra valleḻuttu vīḻvu* um koḷka. {{eruviṉkaṭumai, ceruviṉkaṭumai}} eṉa varum.

iṉṉum ataṉāṉ ē, {T}ammoṭu civaṇi+ tiripiṭaṉ uṭaiya{/T} eṉa valleḻuttiṉkaṇṇatu* āka varaintu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, ammu+ cāriyai iyalpukaṇattukkaṇ peṟuvaṉa koḷka. {{eruvañāṟci, ceruvañāṟci}} eṉa varum.

e-ṭu: {{ūṉkuṟai; ceykai, talai, puṟam}} eṉa varum.

((itu ~um atu))

TE271i

{Z 68}

{C TOL}

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, iṉṉum ataṟku eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappuviti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}akku eṉ cāriyai peṟṟu muṭital um urittu; vaḻakkattāṉ takkavaḻi aṟital{/T}
vaḻakkiṭattu a+ muṭipu takka iṭam aṟika.

{T}takka vaḻi ~aṟital{/T} eṉṟataṉāl, cāriyai peṟṟavaḻi nilaimoḻi ṉakaram vilakkuṇṇātu niṟṟal um muṉ māṭṭēṟṟāl vilakkuṇṭa valleḻuttu+ keṭātu niṟṟal um koḷka.

e-ṭu: {{ūṉakkuṟai; ceykai, talai, puṟam}} eṉa varum.

{T}vaḻakkattāṉa{/T} eṉṟataṉāṉ, iv= īṟṟu urupiṟku+ ceṉṟa cāriyai poruṭkaṇ ceṉṟavaḻi iyaipu valleḻuttu vīḻkka. {{koṇmūviṉ kuḻām}} eṉa varum.

((`āṭūu' `makaṭūu' eṉṉum peyarkaḷ))

TE272i

{Z 69}

{C TOL}
āṭūu makaṭūu āy iru peyarkku* um
iṉ= iṭaivariṉum māṉam illai.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, av= īṟṟu uyartiṇaippeyarkku* um muṉ eytiya valleḻuttu* ē ~aṉṟi, cāriyai ~um peṟum eṉa eytiyataṉ mēl+ ciṟappu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}āṭūu makaṭūu ā iru peyarkku* um{/T}
āṭūu makaṭūu ~ākiya av= iraṇṭu peyarkku* um
{T}iṉ iṭaivariṉum māṉam illai{/T}
mēl {T}kuṟṟeḻuttu* impar um{/T} (([E268i])) eṉṉum cūttirattiṉ {T}niṟṟal{/T} eṉpataṉāl vanta valleḻuttu* ē ~aṉṟi iṉcāriyai iṭai variṉum kuṟṟam illai.

e-ṭu: {{āṭūuviṉkai, makaṭūuviṉkai; cevi, talai, puṟam}} eṉa varum.

{T}māṉamillai{/T} eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṉ peṟṟavaḻi mēl ilēciṉāṉ eytiya valleḻuttu vīḻkka.

((7. ekara okara īṟu))

((alvaḻiyil ekara okara īṟukaḷ))

TE273i

{C TOL}

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, ekaravīṟṟiṟku* um okaravīṟṟiṟku* um īṟu* ākāta nilaiyil vēṟupāṭu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}ekaram okaram peyarkku īṟu ākā{/T}

e-ṭu: {{ēe}} eṉa ~um, {{ōo}} eṉa ~um varum. ivai muṉṉilai viṉai.

{{ēe koṇṭāṉ, ōo koṇṭāṉ}} ivai iṭaiccol.

((ekara okara iṭaiccoṟkaḷ))

{{ēekkoṟṟā, ōokoṟṟā; cāttā, tēvā, pūtā}} eṉa ivai muṉṉilaiviṉai. {T}iyaṟkai{/T} eṉṟataṉāṉ, a+ muṉṉilai viṉaikaḷai aḷapeṭai ~āka niṟīikkoḷka.

nāḷ muṉkūṟātu tiṅkaḷ muṉkūṟiyavataṉāl, {{kariyavaṟṟukkōṭu}} eṉa ~um; {{avaiyattukkoṇṭāṉ}} eṉa ~um; {{vaḻaiṅkōṭu}} eṉa ~um; {{kalaiṅkōṭu, kalaikkōṭu}} eṉa ~um iv= īṟṟu muṭiyātaṉa ~ellām koḷka.

((`maḻai' eṉṉum col))

TE288i

{Z 85}

{C TOL}
maḻai ~eṉ kiḷavi vaḷi ~iyal nilaiyum.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, iyaipu valleḻuttiṉōṭu attu+ pēṟu* um valleḻuttu vilakki iṉṉum vakukkiṉṟamaiyiṉ eytiyataṉ mēṟ ciṟappu* um eytiyatu vilakki+ piṟitum kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.

{T}maḻai eṉ kiḷavi vaḷi iyal nilaiyum{/T}

e-ṭu: {{maḻaiyattu+ koṇṭāṉ, maḻaiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ}} eṉa varum.

((`vēṭkai' muṉ `avā'))

TE289i

{Z 86}

{C TOL}

ai ~eṉ iṟuti avā muṉ variṉ ē

ṭakāram ṇakāram ātal vēṇṭum.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, iv= īṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṟku alvaḻikkaṇ ceyyuḷ muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.

{T}avā muṉvariṉ{/T}

{T}meyyoṭu* um keṭutal eṉmaṉār pulavar{/T}
av= aikāram tāṉ ūrnta meyyōṭu* um^ kūṭa+ keṭuka ~eṉṟu colluvar pulavar; {T}ṭakāram ṇakāram ātal vēṇṭum{/T}

e-ṭu:
«vēṇ avā naliya veyya ~uyirā»
(([naṟṟiṇai:61_2]))

eṉa varum. itaṉai ummaittokai ~āka+ koḷka. avāvu* eṉpatu avvēṭkaiyiṉ mikuti.

iv= alvaḻiyai vēṟṟumai muṭipiṟku muṉ kūṟātataṉāl {{viccāvāti}} eṉṟāṟpōla varum ummaittokai alvaḻi muṭipu* um, pāṟaṅkal eṉa irupeyaroṭṭu alvaḻi muṭipu* um koḷka.

vēṟṟumaipporuḷ+ puṇarccikkaṇ= āyiṉ urupu puṇarccikkaṇ coṉṉa iyalpiṉ kaṇ= ē niṉṟu
cāriyaipeṟuvaṉa īṟu keṭṭu iṭai ~um īṟu* um cāriyai peṟṟu* um
neṭumutal kuṟukuvaṉa neṭumutal kuṟuki ~um muṭiyum
{T}a vayiṉ melleḻuttu mikutal āṉa{/T}
a+ neṭumutal kuṟukumoḻikkaṇ melleḻuttu mikum.

e-ṭu: {{ellārtaṅkaiyum, ellīrnuṅkaiyum; cevi ~um, talai ~um, puṟam um}} eṉa ~um; {{taṅkai, naṅkai, eṅkai; cevi, talai, puṟam}} eṉa ~um oṭṭuka.

{T}vēṟṟumai ~āyiṉ{/T} eṉṟataṉāl, paṭarkkaippeyarkku* um muṉṉilaippeyarkku* um iyalpukaṇattu ñakaram um nakaram um vantavaḻi tammuccāriyai ~um nummuccāriyai ~um īṟu keṭutal koḷka.

{T}āvayiṉāṉa{/T} eṉṟataṉāl, paṭarkkaippeyarkku* um muṉṉilaippeyarkku* um ñakaram um nakaram um vantuḻi avai mikutal um, toṭakkam kuṟukum peyarkku* um añ ñakaram um nakaram um vantuḻi makaram^ keṭṭu avai mikutal um^ koḷka. {{ellār taññāṇum, ellīr nuññāṇum; nūlum}} eṉa ~um; {{taññāṇ, naññāṇ, eññāṇ, nūl}} eṉa ~um varum.

iṉṉum {T}āvayiṉāṉa{/T} eṉṟataṉāl, paṭarkkaippeyar um muṉṉilaippeyar um cāriyai peṟātu iṟuti ummu+ peṟutal um koḷka. {{ellārkaiyum, ellīrkaiyum; cevi ~um, talai ~um, puṟam um}} eṉa varum. iṉṉum ataṉāṉ ē, urupīṟṟu+ ceykai ~ellām koḷka. {{tamakāṇam}} eṉa varum.

{T}iyalpu{/T} eṉṟataṉāl, peyar oṟṟu* um akaram um keṭātu* ē niṉṟu muṭintavāṟu* ē muṭitalum koḷka. {{ātantai, pūtantai}} eṉa varum.

{T}tuvara{/T} eṉṟataṉāl, aḻāṉ, puḻāṉ eṉa niṟutti+ poruntiṉa ceykai ceytu {{aḻāntai puḻāntai}} eṉa muṭikka.

((ataṟku vēṟoru muṭipu))

iṉṉum ataṉāṉ ē, {{akkāṟkoṇṭāṉ, ikkāṟkoṇṭāṉ, ukkāṟkoṇṭāṉ, ekkāṟkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ}} eṉa urupu vārātu urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantavaṟṟiṉ muṭipu* um īṇṭu* ē koḷka.

av= ōcai vēṟṟumai ~āvaṉa, aikāravīṟu* āyavaḻi av= aikārattu mēl oliyūṉṟi ~um, lakāravīṟu* āyavaḻi anta lakāravoṟṟiṉ mēl oliyūṉṟi ~um, anta lakāravīṟu iyalpu muṭipu* āyavaḻi+ paṇputtokai pōla oru tiraṇmai ~āka oliyātu iṭaiyaṟṟu lakārattu mēl oliyūṉṟi ~um, ākāravīṟu* āyavaḻi oru tiraṇmai ~āy av= ākārattu mēl oliyūṉṟi ~um, āṇṭu lakāravīṟu* āyavaḻi oru tiraṇmai ~āka oliyātu anta lakārattu mēl oliyūṉṟi ~um varum vēṟupāṭukaḷ pōlum.

il eṉpatu etirmaṟai viṉaikkuṟippu-muṟṟu viravu-viṉai.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, iv= ōttiṟku+ puṟaṉaṭai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

{WFW}
{T}uṇara+ kūṟiya{/T}
uṇara+ kūṟappaṭṭa puḷḷiyīṟukaḷ
{T}puṇar iyal maruṅkiṉ{/T}
(varumoḻiyōṭu) puṇarum iyalpiṭattu
{T}kaṇṭu ceyaṟku uriyavai kaṇṇiṉar koḷal{/T}
(mēl muṭitta muṭipukaḷ ē ~aṉṟi vaḻakkiṉuḷ) kaṇṭu muṭittaṟku* uriya piṟa muṭipukaḷai+ karutikkoṇṭu muṭikka.

e-ṭu: {{viḻaṉkuḷam; cēṟu, tarai, paḻaṉam}} eṉa ivai, ṉakāra īṟṟu vēṟṟumaikkaṇ tiriyātu iyalpu* āy muṭintaṉa.

{{poṉṉappattam}} eṉpatu av= īṟu akku+ peṟṟu muṭintatu.

{{nīr kuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu}} eṉpatu rakāra īṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu.

{{vēr kuṟitu; vērkkuṟitu}} (eṉpatu) av= īṟṟu alvaḻi uṟaḻcci.

{{amparkkoṇṭāṉ, imparkkoṇṭāṉ. umparkkoṇṭāṉ, emparkkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ}} eṉpaṉa av= īṟṟuḷ urupu vārātu urupiṉ poruḷpaṭa vantavaṟṟiṉ muṭipu.

{{takarkkuṭṭi}} eṉpatu av= īṟṟu irupeyaroṭṭuppaṇputtokai ~ākiya alvaḻi muṭipu.

{{vaṭacārkkūrai, teṉcārkkūrai}} eṉpaṉa av= īṟṟu ilakkaṇattōṭu poruntiya marūu muṭipu.

{{ucilaṅkōṭu, eliyālaṅkōṭu}} eṉpaṉa lakāra īṟṟu ammu+ pēṟu.

{{kallampāṟai}} eṉpatu av= īṟṟu alvaḻi ammu+ pēṟu.

{{aḻalattukkoṇṭāṉ}} eṉpatu av= īṟṟu attu+ pēṟu.

{{aḻukkaṟpōr}} eṉpatu av= īṟṟu alvaḻittiripu.

{{yāḻkuṟitu; ciṟitu, peritu}} eṉpaṉa ḻakāra īṟṟu alvaḻi muṭipu.

{{vīḻkuṟitu, vīḻkkuṟitu}} eṉpaṉa av= īṟṟu alvaḻi uṟaḻcci.

{{tāḻappāvai}} eṉpatu av= īṟṟu alvaḻi akku+ pēṟu.

iṉi {T}uṇarakkūṟiya{/T} eṉṟataṉāl, {{kuḷattiṉpuṟam, marattiṉ puṟam}} eṉa urupiṟku eytiya attōṭu iṉ peṟutal um koḷka.

iṉi+ {T}kaṇṇiṉar{/T} eṉṟataṉāl, {{orunāḷai+ kuḻavi}} eṉa ḷakara īṟu ai eṉṉum cāriyai ~um valleḻuttu* um peṟṟu muṭintaṉa koḷka.

piṟa ~um, iv= ōttiṉ vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉa ~ellām itaṉ akattu muṭittu+ koḷka.

((eṭṭāvatu puḷḷimayaṅkiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.))

e-ṭu: {{uḷ poruḷ, uṇṭu poruḷ}} eṉa ~um; {{uṇṭu kāṇam, uṇṭu cākkāṭu, uṇṭu tāmarai}} eṉa ~um; {{uṇṭuñāṇ; nūl, maṇi, yāḻ, vaṭṭu, aṭai, āṭai}} eṉa ~um varum.

{{uḷporuḷ}} eṉpatu paṇputtokai muṭipu aṉṟu* ō eṉiṉ; aḵtu ōcai oṟṟumaippaṭa+ collum vaḻiyatu; iḵtu, ōcai iṭaiyaṟavupaṭa+ collum vaḻiyatu pōlum/.

īṟu eṉa ~um mey eṉa ~um av= uyirmeyyai+ pirittu+ ceykai ōtiṉamaiyāṉ, av= uyirmeyyiṉai oṟṟumai nayattāṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉṟu ōtiṉāṉ āka+ koḷka.

āṟaṉ maruṅkiṉ īṟu meyyoḻiya+ keṭum eṉṉātu kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉṟu* ōtiṉamaiyāṉ, neṭumutal kuṟukātu* ē niṉṟu {{āṟāyiram}} eṉṟu* um ām.

vaḻaṅkiyal maruṅkiṉ uṇarntaṉar oḻukkal
naṉmati nāṭṭattu* eṉmaṉār pulavar.

{C URAI}

{INTRO} itu, iv= atikāra+ puṟaṉaṭai ~uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

{T}kiḷanta alla ceyyuḷuḷ tirina ~um{/T}
muṉ eṭuttōtiṉa allātaṉa ceyyuḷiṭattu vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavaṟṟai ~um
{T}vaḻaṅku iyal maruṅkiṉ maruvoṭu tirina ~um{/T}
vaḻakku naṭattumiṭattu maruvutalōṭu vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavaṟṟai ~um
{T}viḷampiya iyaṟkaiyiṉ vēṟupaṭa+ tōṉṟiṉ
nalmati nāṭṭattu vaḻaṅku iyal maruṅkiṉ uṇarntaṉar oḻukkal{/T}
nallaṟiviṉatu ārāycciyāṉ vaḻakku iyalumiṭattu avaṟṟiṉ muṭipu vēṟupāṭukaḷai ~aṟintu naṭattuka
{T}eṉmaṉār pulavar{/T}
eṉṟu colluvar pulavar.

e-ṭu: «naṟavaṅkaṇṇi»,

«kaḷḷiyaṅkāṭu»,

«kāṉalamperuntuṟai»
(([aiṅ:158]))

eṉa ivai vēṟṟumaikkaṇ ammu+ peṟṟu muṭintaṉa.


«vēr piṇi vetirattu+ kāl poru naral icai»
(([naṟṟiṇai:62_1]))

eṉpatu rakaravīṟu attu+ peṟṟu muṭintatu. {{muḷavumā, piṇavunāy}} eṉpaṉa, alvaḻikkaṇ meṉkaṇattu+ kuṟiyataṉ iṟuti+ ciṉai keṭṭu ukaram peṟṟu muṭintaṉa.


«añcevi niṟaiya mantiram^ kūṟi»
(([ANONYME]))

eṉpatu akam eṉṉum nilaimoḻi cevi eṉṉum varumoḻiyōṭu vēṟupaṭa muṭintatu.

`āyiṭai' eṉpatu av+ eṉṉum vakaravīṟu vēṟupaṭa muṭintatu.

{{taṭavuttirai, taṭavuttōḷ}} eṉpaṉa uriccol muṭipu.

{{arumaruntaṉṉāṉ}} eṉaṟpālatu {{arumaruntāṉ}} eṉa marūu ~āy muṭintatu. {{cōṇāṭu malāṭu}} eṉpatu* um atu.

{{potiyil}} eṉpatu* um atu. piṟa ~um aṉṉa.

((oṉpatāvatu kuṟṟiyalukara+ puṇariyal muṟṟiṟṟu.))
